I wouldn't use a strong word like distress. But... having to apologize after those meetings, and... my friend telling me to step back from the sign-up table because I was making people tense... I suppose that's an interference. It's just... not ideal. That's all.\n\nDoctor: Do you find it hard to focus or stay attentive during conversations or tasks, even when there's nothing else going on around you?\n\nPatient: Well... no, I don't think so. I can follow a conversation just fine. I haven't really noticed any trouble with that.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find yourself acting on impulse without thinking things through, or do you have trouble waiting your turn in situations?\n\nPatient: Well... I suppose waiting can be a bit bothersome. Like... if I'm in the sign-up line, or when they're handing out supplies. I get a bit fidgety. \n\nI might catch myself sighing, or... checking my watch. Sometimes I end up edging forward when I really should just stand there and wait my turn. I get a bit short, I guess. But... that's about it.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find yourself easily distracted by things around you, even when you're trying to focus on something important?\n\nPatient: No... not really. If I need to focus on something, I just do. I haven't noticed any... particular problems with being distracted.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
